```
    g = compute weights cross
```

Implement `_log_lbfgs_obj(y, beta, k, mode, scale)`:
- n = len(y); a = exp(y - max)/sum.
- c = self_conv; d = n*c
- M = d.max()
- if mode startswith 'top': K=max(1,min(k,len(d))); idx=argpartition(d, len(d)-K)[-K:]; dA=d[idx]; M=dA.max(); z=beta*(dA-M); e=exp(z); Se=sum; L=M+logSe/beta; wA=e/Se; if K small we can compute cross direct with idx,wA.

- cross = ... sum weights 1.
- dot = a.dot(cross)
- gy = scale * 4.0 * n * a * (cross - dot)
- val = scale * 2.0 * L
Return val, gy

Let's code `_log_loop` carefully:
```
def _log_loop(a, best_a, best_sc, deadline, mode, beta, K, eta_values, label, min_improve=1e-14):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    it=0; no=0
    last_print=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        c = _self_conv(a)
        sc = _score(a,c)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
        res, res_sc = _log_ls_step(a,c,mode,beta,K,eta_values)
        it += 1
        if res is not None and res_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            a = res; best_sc = res_sc; best_a = res.copy(); no=0
        else:
            no += 1
        if it == 1 or it % 200 == 0 or (sc < last_print_score - 1e-6):
             print...
        if no > 120:
             break
    return best_sc,best_a
```
But if res_sc < best but not current? res from line search relative to a, best_sc maybe from earlier. Accept if res_sc < best_sc. Good. If no improve, no. If many no, maybe current a is not best? We keep a as current; if best_a different (due L-BFGS?) In refine we pass a=best_a each stage. In loop if candidate not best, best_a remains. At end return best_a. If no_improve, break.

```
def _cross_top(a, idx, w, threshold=64):
    n=a.size
    if len(idx) <= threshold:
        return _cross_top_direct...
    M=2*n-1
```

```
g=np.zeros(n)
for k, weight in zip(idx,w):

    i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)+1
    j0=k-(i1-1); j1=k-i0+1
    g[i0:i1] += wt * a[j0:j1]
return g
```

```
def _lse_full_info(d,beta):
    M=float(d.max())
    z=beta*(d-M)
    # clip z to [-745, 0] maybe
    z=np.where(z < -745, -745.0, z)

    L=M + math.log(Se)/beta
    w=e/Se
    return L,w
```

Now, a-space L-BFGS top objective:
```
def _lbfgs_a_obj(x, beta, k):
    a = np.asarray(x, float)
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-10: return 1e12, zeros
    c=_self_conv(a)
    d=float(n)*c/(S*S)
    M=len(d)
    K=max(1,min(k,M))
    if K >= M: full info? But a-space use top for speed; if k large, maybe full. Simpler top; if K=M full? top argpartition all.
    if K == M: die full
    idx=np.argpartition(d, M-K)[-K:]
    dA=d[idx]; dmax=dA.max()
    z=beta*(dA-dmax); e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-745,0)); Se=e.sum(); L=dmax+math.log(Se)/beta; ws=e/Se
    corr=_cross_top(a, idx, ws, threshold=64)
    LDS=float(ws @ dA)
    grad=4.0*float(n)*corr/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S
    PEN=1e-3
    val=2.0*L + PEN*(S-1.0)**2
    grad=grad + 2.0*PEN*(S-1.0) * np.ones_like(grad) ? Wait derivative of PEN(S-1)^2 wrt x_i = 2PEN(S-1). Yes scalar.
    return float(val), grad
```
But if S not 1, L uses d normalized, score approximate. Adding penalty okay. In `_run_lbfgs_a`, start a normalized. Bounds nonneg. Could hit zeros, log? okay.

```
def _top_idx(c, K):
    m=c.size
    K=int(max(1,min(K,m)))
    if K>=m:
       return np.arange(m)

    idx=np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:]
```

```
def _best_shift_sequence(a, rng, max_shifts=128):
    n=len(a)
    if n<64: return a
    best=a; best_sc=score
    shifts = np.unique([0,int(s) for s in np.linspace(0,n-1,min(n,max_shifts),dtype=int)] + [rng.integers(0,n, size=64)])
    for s in shifts:
       if s==0: continue
       b=np.roll(a,s)
       sc=score(b)  # _score computes conv; okay
       if sc<best_sc: update
    return best
```

Now, let's detail functions code.

`_safe_norm`:
```
def _safe_norm(a, eps=1e-10):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)/16.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S
    if a.size > 0:
        a = a + (eps / a.size)
        a /= a.sum()
    return a
```
If eps=1e-10, total add 1e-10; okay.

`_score`:
```
def _score(a, c=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    if c is None: c=_self_conv(a)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M<0: M=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```

`_self_conv`:
```
def _self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # Clip tiny negatives
    out[out < 0] = 0.0 # inefficient? Use np.maximum
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
Use `return np.maximum(out, 0.0).astype`? okay.

`_cross_full`: as above. Note if `w` size not M? ensure m=2n-1.
```
def _cross_full(a,w):
   n=a.size; M=2*n-1; w=np.asarray(w,float)[:M]
   N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   ar=a[::-1]
   A=np.fft.rfft(ar,N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
   return np.nan_to_num(full[n-1:2*n-1], nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
If n=1, 3n-2=1, N=1; slice n-1:2n-1 ->0:1 okay.

`_cross_top_direct`: loop. If weight array maybe complex? no.
```
for i in range(len(idx)):
  k=int(idx[i]); wt=float(ws[i]); if wt==0 skip
  i0 = max(0, k - n + 1); i1 = min(n - 1, k) + 1
  if i0 >= i1: continue
  j0 = k - (i1 - 1); j1 = k - i0 + 1
  g[i0:i1] += wt * a[j0:j1]
```
Need ensure j0,j1 within [0,n]. Since i0,i1 valid, yes.

`_cross_top`:
```
def _cross_top(a, idx, ws, threshold=64):
   if len(idx)==0: return zeros
   if len(idx) <= threshold: direct
   M=2*len(a)-1
   wf=np.zeros(M)
   wf[idx]=ws
   return _cross_full(a,wf)
```
If idx has duplicates? no. If ws length != len idx? ensure.

`_cross_weighted`:
```
def _cross_weighted(a,w,idx=None,ws=None):
   if idx is not None: return _cross_top(a,idx,ws)
   nz=np.nonzero(w > 1e-15)[0]
   if nz.size <= 64: return _cross_top(a,nz,w[nz])
   return _cross_full(a,w)
```
If w has negative? no.

`_top_idx` and `_lse_top_arrays`:
```
def _lse_top_arrays(c,beta,K):
   m=c.size; K=int(max(1,min(K,m)))
   if beta <= 0 or K == 1:
      idx=_top_idx(c,K)
      return idx, np.full(K,1.0/K)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, m-K)[m-K:]
   dA=c[idx]
   dmax=float(dA.max())
   if dmax <= 0:
      ws=np.full(K,1/K); return idx,ws
   z=beta*(dA-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z, -36.0, 0.0) # don't underflow, but if beta large, small weights zero; okay. -36 corresponds 2e-16.
   e=np.exp(z)
   Se=float(e.sum())
   if Se <= 0 or not np.isfinite(Se): ws=np.full(K,1/K); return idx,ws
   ws=e/Se
   return idx, ws
```
For LSE value, need actual e sum maybe using clip -745? If clip -36, log(Se) wrong if many below -36? But for weights gradient, negligible. For value, better compute with -745. We'll separate `_lse_top_info` with -745 and fallback; and `_lse_top_arrays` can use `_lse_top_info` to return weights. To avoid duplication, implement `_lse_top_info(c,beta,K)` returns idx,ws,L. Then arrays call it. Use -745 and fallback. But if Se<1e-300 fallback uniform.
```
def _lse_top_info(c,beta,K):
  m=c.size; K=...
  if beta<=0 or K==1:
     idx=_top_idx(c,K); ws=uniform; L=c[idx].max()
     return idx,ws,L
  idx=np.argpartition(c,m-K)[m-K:]
  dA=c[idx]; dmax=float(dA.max())
  z=beta*(dA-dmax)
  z=np.clip(z,-745.0,0.0)
  e=np.exp(z); Se=float(e.sum())
  if Se < 1e-300 or not finite:
      ws=uniform; L=dmax; return idx,ws,L
  L=dmax + math.log(Se)/beta
  ws=e/Se
  return idx,ws,L
```
For fallback if only max, L=dmax (since LSE of one? If many underflow but Se<, approximate max). Good.

`_lse_full_info(c,beta)`: similar; if beta<=0, uniform weights over all? But log loop full beta positive. Uniform over all not descent? If beta=0, max? Not. Use onehot? For beta=0 full not used. If beta<=0: idx argmax, w onehot, L=M.
```
z=beta*(c-M); clip -745; e=exp; Se=sum; if Se<1e-300 fallback onehot; else w=e/Se; L=M+log(Se)/beta
```
Support maybe one.

`_soft_weights` can just call `_lse_full_info` and return w, but for log loop we don't need L. Use.

`_log_ls_step`:
```
def _log_ls_step(a,c,mode,beta,K,eta_values):
    n=a.size
    if mode=='full':
       _, w, _ = _lse_full_info(c,beta)
       cross = _cross_weighted(a,w)
    elif mode=='top':
       idx, ws, _ = _lse_top_info(c,beta,K)
       cross = _cross_top(a, idx, ws)
    else: return None,np.inf
    g = cross - np.mean(cross)
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if maxd <= 1e-18: return None,np.inf
    base_log = np.log(a)  # positive
    best_q=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for eta in eta_values:
       eta=float(eta)
       if eta <= 0: continue
       y = base_log - eta * g / maxd
       ym=float(np.max(y))
       if not np.isfinite(ym): continue
       q=np.exp(y-ym)
       S=float(q.sum())
       if S <= 0 or not finite: continue
       q=q/S
       sc=_score(q)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_q=q
    return best_q,best_sc
```
If eta_values include too large causing q distribution extreme, logsoftmax stable. Good.

But if base_log has very small a (1e-20), log -46; y - max; exp okay. If a zero? safe_norm prevents.

`_log_loop`:
```
def _log_loop(a,best_a,best_sc,deadline,mode,beta,K,eta_values,label=''):
    a=_safe_norm(a); it=0; no=0; start_s=best_sc
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       c=_self_conv(a); sc=_score(a,c)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       step, step_sc = _log_ls_step(a,c,mode,beta,K,eta_values)
       it+=1
       if step is not None and step_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
          a=step; best_sc=step_sc; best_a=step.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no+=1
       if it == 1 or it % 100 == 0 or (best_sc < start_s - 1e-6):
           print(f"    {label} it={it} sc={best_sc:.8f}")
       if no > 100: break
    # final eval?
    return best_sc, best_a
```
If deadline too close, no iterations. Print too frequent? 100 iters maybe okay. Could print when improvement >1e-7 to avoid spam. Use both.

In `_log_loop`, if mode top K may be larger than c size; `_lse_top_info` handles.

Run L-BFGS log:
`_log_lbfgs_obj` detailed. Need pass `k` and mode. If K maybe 0 for full; top K. For top, K int. Use `_lse_top_info` with d (not c) because L based on d=n*c. But weights same regardless scaling? L value scales with n. If weights based on d, same as c since positive scaling. So can use c. But L value should be based on d. Use d to compute L. For top, dA=n*cA. If LSE of d, L=n * LSE(c) = n*(cmax+log.../beta). The beta then effective? If weights based on d and beta same, z=beta*n*(c-cmax), which differs. Our `_lse_top_info` with input d uses beta*d. So call with d. Good. In `_log_lbfgs_obj`, d = n*c. Use `_lse_top_info(d,beta,K)` / full. In log loop, we can use c directly or d? For direction weights, scaling by n doesn't change weights if beta unchanged? It changes beta effective. For consistency with LSE, we should use d and same beta? def _log_lbfgs_obj(y,beta,k,mode,scale):
```
    y=np.asarray(y,float)
    n=y.size
    if not finite: return 1e12, zeros
    zm=y.max(); a=np.exp(y-zm); S=a.sum(); if S<=0...; a/=S
    c=_self_conv(a); d=float(n)*c
    if mode=='full': L,w=_lse_full_info(d,beta); cross=_cross_weighted(a,w)
    else: idx,ws,L=_lse_top_info(d,beta,int(k)); cross=_cross_top(a,idx,ws)
    dot=float(a.dot(cross))
    gy = scale * 4.0 * float(n) * a * (cross - dot)
    val = scale * 2.0 * L
    # reg
    regcoef=1e-10 * scale? If reg too big? Use 1e-12 * scale.
    val += (1e-12 * scale) * float(np.dot(y,y))
    gy += 2.0 * (1e-12 * scale) * y
    return float(val), gy
```
Wait reg in objective with y scale: If y centered ~0, dot y small. Good.

But `scale * 2L` maybe large. `_log_lbfgs_obj` value not same as score but okay. Initialize y0 centered, reg keeps near.

```
def _run_log_lbfgs(a,beta,k,mode,time_limit,maxfun=1000):
   a=_safe_norm(a)
   init_sc=_score(a)
   if time_limit < 15: return init_sc,a
   y=np.log(a); y-=np.mean(y)
   n=a.size
   scale=max(1.0, n/10.0)
   est=0.05*max(0.5, float(n)/8192.0)
   mf=int(0.85*time_limit/max(est,0.02))
   mf=max(40,min(mf,maxfun,1600))
   maxiter=max(30,mf//3)
   try:
      res=minimize(_log_lbfgs_obj, y, args=(beta,int(k),mode,scale), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':mf,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':40})
      newy=res.x
   except Exception:
      newy=y
   newy=np.nan_to_num(newy, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   a_new=_safe_norm(_softmax(newy))  # use helper
   sc=_score(a_new)
   if sc < init_sc: return sc,a_new
   return init_sc,a
```

```
def _softmax(y):
   y=np.nan_to_num(y,...)
   ym=np.max(y)
   e=np.exp(y-ym)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: uniform
   return e/s
```

```
def _l2_subgrad_polish(a0, deadline, K=32):
   a=_safe_norm(a0); best...
   while time<deadline-2:
      c=_self_conv(a); sc=_score(a,c); update
      idx=_top_idx(c,K); ws=uniform
      cross=_cross_top(a,idx,ws)
      grad=2.0*cross
      d=grad - np.mean(grad)
      gn=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if gn<1e-15: break
      u=d/gn
      best_q=None; best_qs=best_sc
      for scale in (0.0005,0.002,0.008,0.02,0.08,0.2,0.5):
         q=a - scale*u
         q=np.clip(q,0,None); S=q.sum(); if S<1e-8 continue; q/=S
         qs=_score(q)
         if qs<best_qs: best_qs=qs; best_q=q.copy()
      if best_q is not None and best_qs < best_sc - 1e-14:
         a=best_q; best_sc=...; no=0
      else:
         no+=1
      if no>80: break
   return best_sc,best_a
```
Maybe scale 0.5 too large for n small? It clips, safe.

Now, `_best_shift_sequence` uses _score for each shift. If n large, max_shifts limited. We need top-level with rng.
```
def _best_shift_sequence(a, max_shifts=48, rng=None):
    a=_safe_norm(a); n=a.size
    if n<16 or max_shifts<=1: return a, _score(a)
    ms=min(max_shifts,n)
    shifts=list(np.unique(np.linspace(0,n-1,ms,dtype=int).tolist()))
    if rng is not None:
       shifts.extend(int(x) for x in rng.integers(0,n,size=min(16,n)))
    best=a.astype(copy); best_sc=_score(a)
    seen=set([0])
    for s in shifts:
       s=int(s)%n
       if s in seen: continue
       seen.add(s)
       b=np.roll(a,s)
       sc=_score(b)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15: best=b; best_sc=sc
    return _safe_norm(best.copy()), best_sc
```
Using `list(generator)` fine. No lambda.

```
n=a.size; N=_next_pow2(n) maybe rfft length n? Use N=n (rfft of sequence length n) for smoothing, but convolution uses n. Take freqs. np.rfft(a, n) length n//2+1. Keep indices <= keep_frac*(n//2). Set high to 0. irfft length n. positive norm.
```
If keep_frac 1 no change. Use N=n if n even? rfft(n) works. For n odd? okay.

`_smooth_perturb`:
```
def _smooth_perturb(a,rng,strength=0.1):
    n=a.size
    k=max(5,min(max(5,n//200+1),101))
    if k%2==0: k+=1
    if k>n: k=n if n%2==1 else n-1
    c=k//2
    sig=max(1.0,c/3.0)
    kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig)); kern/=kern.sum()
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
    q=a + strength*noise
    return _safe_norm(q)
```
`np.convolve` direct O(n*k), k small; okay.

Now, verify constants in `_log_lbfgs_obj`: value = scale*2L; L approx max d. Score =2 max d. So value/scale approx score. Gradient wrt y = scale*4n*a(cross-dot). Let's verify derivative of value/scale =2L wrt y =4n*a(cross-dot). Good. So scaled objective gradient consistent. Good.

For full LSE with beta, derivative of L: w over all. LSE L = log∑exp(β d)/β. ∂L/∂d_k=w_k. d_k=n c_k. ∂L/∂a_i = n∑w_k 2cross =2n cross. ∂(2L)/∂a=4n cross. gy=a(4n cross - mean). correct.

For top LSE, weights only top K. L approx max over top. Same.

For a-space L-BFGS, previous formula with S. Let's ensure value and grad consistent with penalty. L = LSE of d=n c/S². Objective 2L. Derivative for high beta approx earlier. For low beta, derivative? L=(1/β)log∑exp(β d_k) with d_k=n c_k/S². ∂L/∂a: derivative of d_k: n/S² ∂c - 2 n c_k/S^3 ∂S. Summed w: w terms. ∂c/∂a_i=2cross_i. ∂S/∂a_i=1. So ∂L/∂a=2n/S² cross_i - 2n (∑w d? actually ∑w d_k)/S? Let's derive second: d_k=n c_k/S². ∂L/∂a=∑w [n/S² ∂c_k - 2 d_k/S *1]? Because d_k/S? ∂(n c_k S^{-2}) = n/S² ∂c_k -2n c_k S^{-3}. Multiply ∑w: second term -2 n (w·c)/S^3 = -2 (w·d)/S? since w·d = n w·c/S², so -2 n w·c/S^3 = -2 (w·d)/S. ∂c term = n/S² *2 cross =2n cross/S². So ∂L = (2n/S²) cross - 2 (w·d)/S. ∂(2L)=4n/S² cross -4 (w·d)/S. That matches previous with LDS=w@d. Good. For value, they return 2L. Penalty added. Good.

`_lbfgs_a_obj`: `LDS = float(ws @ dA)`, dA values. `grad=4.0*n*corr/(S*S) - 4.0*LDS/S`. Good. value=2L. Add pen.

```
def _lowpass(a, keep_frac=0.3):
    a=_safe_norm(a); n=a.size
    if n<8 or keep_frac>=1: return a
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,n)
    m=len(F)
    keep=max(1,int(m*keep_frac))
    F[keep:]=0.0
    out=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
    out=out-out.min() # may make negative? Actually if min negative, shift to positive; but spectral signal may mean zero. Use clip 0.
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return _safe_norm(out)
```

Now, one subtlety: Convolution in objective `np.convolve(sequence, sequence)` for length n has output length 2n-1 with coefficients ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} a_i a_{k-i}. Our `_self_conv` via FFT A*A with full linear convolution of a with a (no reverse). Yes same. Good.


```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
with emphasis on improving a good incumbent (height_sequence_1).

Main ingredients:
- Cheap cyclic-roll / symmetry / spectral / low-pass initialization.
- Log-domain projected-gradient / line-search using smooth LSE active weights.
- Log-parameter L-BFGS on LSE objectives (full or active top-k).
- A-space L-BFGS LSE as a complementary local polish.
- Final L2 subgradient polish.
- Multi-length warm starts (incumbent length, next power of two, double).

All heavy work is FFT-based. No nested functions or lambdas are used.
"""
import math
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from operator import itemgetter


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _safe_norm(a, eps=1e-10):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(16, 1.0 / 16.0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / S
    if a.size > 0:
        a = a + (eps / float(a.size))
        a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def _self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _score(a, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if c is None:
        c = _self_conv(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return _safe_norm(a)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _safe_norm(out)


def _softmax(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64) / 16.0
    ym = float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y - ym)
    S = float(e.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / S


def _cross_full(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size > M:
        w = w[:M]
    elif w.size < M:
        tmp = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:w.size] = w
        w = tmp
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    return np.nan_to_num(full[n - 1:2 * n - 1], nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def _cross_top_direct(a, idx, ws):
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
        k = int(idx[ii])
        wt = float(ws[ii])
        if wt == 0.0:
            continue
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(n - 1, k) + 1
        if i0 >= i1:
            continue
        j0 = k - (i1 - 1)
        j1 = k - i0 + 1
        g[i0:i1] += wt * a[j0:j1]
    return g


def _cross_top(a, idx, ws, threshold=64):
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    ws = np.asarray(ws, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx.size <= threshold:
        return _cross_top_direct(a, idx, ws)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    wf = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    wf[idx] = ws
    return _cross_full(a, wf)


def _cross_weighted(a, w, idx=None, ws=None):
    if idx is not None:
        return _cross_top(a, idx, ws)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    nz = np.nonzero(w > 1e-15)[0]
    if nz.size <= 64:
        return _cross_top(a, nz, w[nz])
    return _cross_full(a, w)


def _top_idx(c, K):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    K = int(max(1, min(K, m)))
    if K >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    kth = m - K
    return np.argpartition(c, kth)[kth:].astype(np.int64)


def _lse_top_info(d, beta, K):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = d.size
    K = int(max(1, min(K, m)))
    if beta <= 0.0 or K == 1:
        idx = _top_idx(d, K)
        ws = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        L = float(d[idx].max()) if idx.size else 0.0
        return idx, ws, L
    idx = np.argpartition(d, m - K)[m - K:].astype(np.int64)
    dA = d[idx]
    dmax = float(dA.max())
    if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
        ws = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, ws, 0.0
    z = beta * (dA - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Se = float(e.sum())
    if Se < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(Se):
        ws = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, ws, dmax
    L = dmax + math.log(Se) / float(beta)
    ws = e / Se
    return idx, ws, float(L)


def _lse_full_info(d, beta):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = d.size
    if m == 0:
        return 0.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        j = int(np.argmax(d))
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[j] = 1.0
        return float(d[j]), w
    M = float(d.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
        return 0.0, w
    z = beta * (d - M)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    Se = float(e.sum())
    if Se < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(Se):
        j = int(np.argmax(d))
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[j] = 1.0
        return M, w
    L = M + math.log(Se) / float(beta)
    w = e / Se
    return float(L), w


def _log_ls_step(a, c, mode, beta, K, eta_values):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    d = float(n) * c

    if mode == "full":
        _, w, _ = _lse_full_info(d, beta)
        cross = _cross_weighted(a, w)
    elif mode == "top":
        K = int(max(1, K))
        idx, ws, _ = _lse_top_info(d, beta, K)
        cross = _cross_top(a, idx, ws)
    else:
        return None, float("inf")

    g = cross - float(np.mean(cross))
    maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if maxd <= 1e-18:
        return None, float("inf")

    base_log = np.log(a)
    best_q = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    for eta in eta_values:
        eta = float(eta)
        if eta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(eta):
            continue
        y = base_log - eta * g / maxd
        ym = float(np.max(y))
        if not np.isfinite(ym):
            continue
        q = np.exp(y - ym)
        S = float(q.sum())
        if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
            continue
        q = q / S
        sc = _score(q)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_q = q
    return best_q, best_sc


def _log_loop(a, best_a, best_sc, deadline, mode, beta, K, eta_values, label=""):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    start_score = float(best_sc)
    print(f"      {label} start {best_sc:.8f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.5:
        c = _self_conv(a)
        sc = _score(a, c)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        q, qsc = _log_ls_step(a, c, mode, beta, K, eta_values)
        it += 1

        if q is not None and qsc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            a = q
            best_sc = qsc
            best_a = q.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if it == 1 or it % 150 == 0 or best_sc < start_score - 1e-6:
            print(f"      {label} it={it} score={best_sc:.8f}")
        if no_improve > 60:
            break

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        sc = _score(a)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
    print(f"      {label} end   {best_sc:.8f}")
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def _log_lbfgs_obj(y, beta, k, mode, scale):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e12, np.empty(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = _softmax(y)
    c = _self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c

    if mode == "full":
        L, w = _lse_full_info(d, beta)
        cross = _cross_weighted(a, w)
    else:
        K = int(max(1, int(k)))
        idx, ws, L = _lse_top_info(d, beta, K)
        cross = _cross_top(a, idx, ws)

    dot = float(a.dot(cross))
    gy = max(1.0, float(scale)) * 4.0 * float(n) * a * (cross - dot)

    val = max(1.0, float(scale)) * 2.0 * L

    rho = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(scale))
    val += rho * float(np.dot(y, y))
    gy += 2.0 * rho * y

    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gy))
    if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm > 1e6:
        gy = gy / max(gnorm, 1.0) * 1e6

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), np.nan_to_num(gy, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)


def _run_log_lbfgs(a, beta, k, mode, time_limit, maxfun=1200):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    init_sc = _score(a)
    if time_limit <= 15.0:
        return init_sc, a

    y = np.log(a)
    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    n = a.size
    scale = max(1.0, float(n) / 10.0)

    est = 0.08 * max(0.5, float(n) / 8192.0)
    mf = int(0.85 * time_limit / max(est, 0.02))
    mf = max(40, min(mf, maxfun, 1800))
    maxiter = max(30, mf // 3)

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _log_lbfgs_obj,
            y,
            args=(float(beta), int(k), str(mode), float(scale)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": mf,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "maxls": 45,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        newy = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        newy = y

    a_new = _softmax(newy)
    sc_new = _score(a_new)
    if sc_new < init_sc:
        return sc_new, a_new
    return init_sc, a


def _lbfgs_a_obj(x, beta, k):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e12, np.empty(0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-10:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = _self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c / (S * S)

    if k <= 0:
        L, w = _lse_full_info(d, beta)
        corr = _cross_weighted(a, w)
        LDS = float(w.dot(d))
    else:
        K = int(max(1, int(k)))
        idx, ws, L = _lse_top_info(d, beta, K)
        corr = _cross_top(a, idx, ws)
        LDS = float(ws.dot(d[idx]))

    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * corr / (S * S) - 4.0 * LDS / S

    PEN = 1e-3
    val = 2.0 * L + PEN * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = grad + 2.0 * PEN * (S - 1.0)

    gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm > 1e6:
        grad = grad / max(gnorm, 1.0) * 1e6

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)


def _run_lbfgs_a(a, beta, k, time_limit, maxfun=900):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    init_sc = _score(a)
    if time_limit <= 15.0:
        return init_sc, a

    n = a.size
    est = 0.10 * max(0.5, float(n) / 8192.0)
    mf = int(0.85 * time_limit / max(est, 0.02))
    mf = max(35, min(mf, maxfun, 1100))
    maxiter = max(30, mf // 3)

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lbfgs_a_obj,
            a,
            args=(float(beta), int(k)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": mf,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "maxls": 45,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        x = a

    a_new = _safe_norm(x)
    sc_new = _score(a_new)
    if sc_new < init_sc:
        return sc_new, a_new
    return init_sc, a


def _l2_subgrad_polish(a0, deadline, K=32):
    a = _safe_norm(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    no_improve = 0
    K = int(max(1, K))

    scales = (0.0005, 0.002, 0.008, 0.02, 0.08, 0.2, 0.5)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.5:
        c = _self_conv(a)
        sc = _score(a, c)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = _top_idx(c, K)
        ws = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
        cross = _cross_top(a, idx, ws)
        grad = 2.0 * cross
        d = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if gn < 1e-15:
            break
        u = d / gn

        cand = None
        cand_sc = best_sc
        for s in scales:
            q = a - float(s) * u
            q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
            S = float(q.sum())
            if S < 1e-8:
                continue
            q = q / S
            qs = _score(q)
            if qs < cand_sc - 1e-15:
                cand_sc = qs
                cand = q

        if cand is not None and cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            a = cand
            best_sc = cand_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 60:
            break

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    n = a.size
    k = max(3, int(n // 200) + 1)
    k = min(k, 51)
    if k >= n:
        k = max(1, n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1)
    if k <= 0:
        k = 1
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    c = k // 2
    sig = max(1.0, float(c) / 2.0)
    kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    kern /= kern.sum()
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    q = a + float(strength) * noise
    return _safe_norm(q)


def _spectral_init(n, rng):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    Nf = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
    exponent = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.35))
    power = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.abs(freqs)) ** exponent
    if power.size:
        power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
    spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
    sig = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
    sig = np.maximum(sig, 0.0)
    S = float(sig.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return _safe_norm(sig)


def _lowpass(a, keep_frac=0.30):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or keep_frac >= 1.0:
        return a
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, n)
    m = F.size
    keep = max(1, int(m * keep_frac))
    F[keep:] = 0.0
    out = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    out = out - float(np.min(out))
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return _safe_norm(out)


def _best_shift_sequence(a, max_shifts=32):
    a = _safe_norm(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or max_shifts <= 1:
        return a, _score(a)

    ms = int(max(2, min(int(max_shifts), n)))
    shifts = np.unique(np.linspace(0, n - 1, ms, dtype=int).astype(np.int64))

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    seen = set()

    for s in shifts:
        s = int(s) % n
        if s in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(s)
        b = np.roll(a, s)
        sc = _score(b)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best = b

    return _safe_norm(best), float(best_sc)


def _refine_sequence(a0, deadline, label=""):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = deadline - t0
    a_init = _safe_norm(a0)
    init_sc = _score(a_init)
    if budget < 10.0:
        return init_sc, a_init

    a = a_init.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = init_sc

    if budget > 200.0:
        n = a.size
        if n < 4096:
            ms = 64
        elif n < 8192:
            ms = 48
        elif n < 16384:
            ms = 32
        else:
            ms = 16
        if budget < 500.0:
            ms //= 2
        a_sh, sc_sh = _best_shift_sequence(a, ms)
        if sc_sh < best_sc - 1e-15:
            a = a_sh
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc_sh

    print(f"    [{label}] n={len(a)} budget={budget:.0f}s start={best_sc:.8f}")

    n = len(a)
    Mc = 2 * n - 1

    eta_full = (0.05, 0.2, 0.8)
    eta_top = (0.02, 0.1, 0.4, 1.5)
    eta_small = (0.01, 0.05, 0.2, 1.0)

    specs = []

    for beta in (20.0, 80.0, 300.0):
        specs.append(("log", "full", beta, 0, 0.05, eta_full))

    top_kfs = (0.15, 0.10, 0.06, 0.035, 0.02, 0.012, 0.006, 0.003)
    top_betas = (20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0, 400.0, 800.0, 1500.0, 3000.0)
    for kf, bt in zip(top_kfs, top_betas):
        K = int(max(2, min(4096, int(Mc * kf))))
        specs.append(("log", "top", bt, K, 0.02, eta_top))

    for beta in (200.0, 800.0, 3000.0, 10000.0):
        specs.append(("lbf_log", "full", beta, 0, 0.05, None))

    lbf_top_pairs = (
        (0.05, 800.0),
        (0.025, 2000.0),
        (0.012, 4000.0),
        (0.006, 8000.0),
        (0.003, 15000.0),
        (0.0015, 30000.0),
    )
    for kf, bt in lbf_top_pairs:
        K = int(max(4, min(4096, int(Mc * kf))))
        specs.append(("lbf_log", "top", bt, K, 0.025, None))

    specs.append(("log", "top", 3000.0, 8, 0.015, eta_small))
    specs.append(("log", "top", 5000.0, 16, 0.015, eta_small))

    for kf, bt in ((0.02, 2000.0), (0.005, 8000.0)):
        K = int(max(8, min(2048, int(Mc * kf))))
        specs.append(("lbf_a", "top", bt, K, 0.03, None))

    specs.append(("l2", "top", 0.0, 32, 0.04, None))

    total_frac = 0.0
    for spec in specs:
        total_frac += float(spec[4])
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    cum = 0.0
    for spec in specs:
        typ, mode, beta, K, frac0, eta_vals = spec
        frac = float(frac0) / total_frac
        cum += frac
        phase_end = min(t0 + cum * budget, deadline - 5.0)
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > phase_end - 3.0:
            continue

        K = int(K) if K is not None else 0
        beta = float(beta)

        if typ == "log":
            if now < phase_end - 5.0:
                sc, arr = _log_loop(
                    a, best_a, best_sc, phase_end,
                    mode=mode, beta=beta, K=K,
                    eta_values=eta_vals if eta_vals is not None else eta_top,
                    label=f"{label}/{mode}",
                )
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = arr.copy()
                a = _safe_norm(best_a)

        elif typ == "lbf_log":
            time_limit = phase_end - now
            if time_limit > 18.0:
                sc, arr = _run_log_lbfgs(a, beta, K, mode, time_limit, maxfun=900)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = arr.copy()
                a = _safe_norm(arr)

        elif typ == "lbf_a":
            time_limit = phase_end - now
            if time_limit > 18.0:
                sc, arr = _run_lbfgs_a(a, beta, K, time_limit, maxfun=700)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = arr.copy()
                a = _safe_norm(arr)

        elif typ == "l2":
            sc, arr = _l2_subgrad_polish(a, phase_end, K if K > 0 else 32)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = arr.copy()
            a = _safe_norm(arr)

    final_sc = _score(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
        best_sc = final_sc
        best_a = a.copy()

    print(f"    [{label}] final {best_sc:.8f}")
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(max(120.0, budget_s))
    t_end_safe = t_start + max(120.0, budget_s - 45.0)
    work_end = t_end_safe - 30.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                MAX_N = 16384
                if p.size > MAX_N:
                    p = _upsample(p, MAX_N)
                parent = _safe_norm(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n_base = 8192
        base_arr = _spectral_init(n_base, rng)
    else:
        n_base = parent.size
        base_arr = parent.copy()

    if n_base > 64:
        base_arr, _ = _best_shift_sequence(base_arr, 64 if n_base < 8192 else 32)

    inits = []
    inits.append(("base", base_arr.copy()))
    sym = _safe_norm(0.5 * (base_arr + base_arr[::-1]))
    inits.append(("sym", sym))
    for amp in (0.05, 0.15, 0.30):
        inits.append((f"pn{amp}", _smooth_perturb(base_arr, rng, amp)))
    inits.append(("lowpass", _lowpass(base_arr, 0.30)))
    for i in range(3):
        inits.append((f"spec{i}", _spectral_init(n_base, rng)))

    scored = []
    for name, arr in inits:
        arr = _safe_norm(arr)
        sc = _score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, name, arr))

    if not scored:
        scored.append((2.0, "uniform", np.ones(n_base, dtype=np.float64) / n_base))

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    best_init_score, best_init_name, best_init_arr = scored[0]
    print(f"Initial candidates: best={best_init_name} score={best_init_score:.8f}")
    if len(scored) > 1:
        print(f"Second: {scored[1][1]} score={scored[1][0]:.8f}")

    overall_seq = _safe_norm(best_init_arr)
    overall_sc = float(best_init_score)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = work_end - now
    if rem < 60.0:
        result = _safe_norm(overall_seq)
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in result]))
        except Exception:
            verified = overall_sc
        print(f"FINAL: internal={overall_sc:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, len={len(result)}")
        return [float(x) for x in result]

    base_deadline = now + max(60.0, 0.58 * rem)
    sc1, seq1 = _refine_sequence(overall_seq, base_deadline, "base")
    if sc1 < overall_sc - 1e-15:
        overall_sc = sc1
        overall_seq = _safe_norm(seq1)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = work_end - now
    MAX_N = 16384
    n2 = _next_pow2(n_base)
    if n2 <= n_base:
        n2 = 2 * n_base
    n2 = min(int(n2), MAX_N)

    did_n2 = False
    if n2 != n_base and rem > 320.0:
        n2_deadline = now + max(120.0, 0.42 * rem)
        start2 = _upsample(overall_seq, n2)
        sc2, seq2 = _refine_sequence(start2, n2_deadline, f"n{n2}")
        did_n2 = True
        if sc2 < overall_sc - 1e-15:
            overall_sc = sc2
            overall_seq = _safe_norm(seq2)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = work_end - now
    n3 = min(2 * n2, MAX_N)
    if did_n2 and n3 != n2 and rem > 360.0:
        n3_deadline = now + max(120.0, 0.45 * rem)
        start3 = _upsample(overall_seq, n3)
        sc3, seq3 = _refine_sequence(start3, n3_deadline, f"n{n3}")
        if sc3 < overall_sc - 1e-15:
            overall_sc = sc3
            overall_seq = _safe_norm(seq3)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = work_end - now
    if rem > 250.0:
        scf, seqf = _refine_sequence(overall_seq, work_end - 10.0, "final")
        if scf < overall_sc - 1e-15:
            overall_sc = scf
            overall_seq = _safe_norm(seqf)
    elif rem > 60.0:
        scf, seqf = _l2_subgrad_polish(overall_seq, work_end - 10.0, 32)
        if scf < overall_sc - 1e-15:
            overall_sc = scf
            overall_seq = _safe_norm(seqf)

    if work_end - time.monotonic() > 60.0:
        sh, sh_sc = _best_shift_sequence(overall_seq, 16)
        if sh_sc < overall_sc - 1e-15:
            overall_sc = sh_sc
            overall_seq = _safe_norm(sh)

    out = _safe_norm(overall_seq)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out = out / S

    result = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        verified = overall_sc

    print(
        f"FINAL: internal={overall_sc:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, "
        f"len={len(result)}, time={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s"
    )
    return result
```
